# Name-link review

Generated by `node scripts/names/link-review.mjs`. Do not edit this file by hand. `npm run validate` checks that it matches the linker.

The Other people list links a whole name when one person record matches it. A token that matches more than one record stays unlinked unless the Tubal context rule or `data/name-link-overrides.json` selects one record. The linker does not guess.

Tubal in a passage or note about Cain’s line resolves to Tubal-cain. Otherwise it resolves to Japheth’s son. Cain’s line means the unit is linked to, or uniquely mentions, Lamech the Cainite, Zillah, Adah, Naamah, Jubal, Jabal, or Cain, or is linked to Tubal-cain, or uniquely names him Tubalcain or Tubal-cain. A bare Lamech does not count, because two records share that name.

- Tubal resolved to Tubal-cain: 9

- Names that match no record, in the 1617 passages and in summaries and descriptions: 35190 occurrences, 6965 distinct names

## Names in histories that match no record

A name here is a capitalized word of at least three letters, not a function word and not a Roman numeral, that matched no person. Sentence-initial words are included, so the list is wider than personal names. Only the 1617 passages and the summaries and descriptions are listed. Chronology notes and name-form citations are omitted: they repeat our own citation wording. Intake words such as Added, Display, CFAH, and PDF are omitted from summaries and descriptions. `raleigh` is a passage. `prose` is a summary or description.
